# Reporting papers

> 1321 published papers from which ORCHID literature values were read, with DOI, systems and number of values.

HTML: https://data.orchid-project.org/papers/index.html · Markdown: https://data.orchid-project.org/papers/index.md

Each listed paper is a reporting paper: values were read from its tables or running text. The papers themselves are not redistributed; follow the DOI. Number of values counts all extracted records, accepted or not. Full bibliography including cited works: [papers.json](https://data.orchid-project.org/literature/papers.json).

Version 1, machine-extracted: values were read from papers by language-model readers constrained to quote their source and checked automatically (quote verification, field-placement rule, printed-page sign checks, DFT and Kim et al. 2017 cross-checks); a hand-checked precision sample is in progress.
